# Reviewer guide

This guide lists every number printed in the manuscript that the repository recomputes, with the code and the source data table behind it, in v30 manuscript order. The reference text is v30 (`visible_v30f.txt`, one paragraph per line, `<index><TAB><text>`; the index is `v30 para`). Units in `tables/` and `text_statistics/` carry the v30 paragraph, wording, printed value and status in their `NUMBERS.tsv` (columns `v30_*`). Figure units still carry only the v28d line; for them `v30 para` is an approximate paragraph (prefix `~`) found by word overlap with the v28d line, and their values are not yet checked against v30. The table is merged from the `NUMBERS.tsv` file of every unit; do not edit it by hand, edit the unit file and regenerate.

v30 status values: `MATCH` the recomputed value agrees with the v30 printed value under the rounding rules of `tools/check_numbers.py`; `OPEN` it does not agree or was not traced (reason in the unit `OPEN_ITEMS.md`); `NOT_IN_V30` the v28d quote was not located in the v30 text (wording or value changed, or the sentence was removed). v28d status values (`MATCH`, `v28f_change`, `OPEN`) are kept in the unit files.

Totals: 1354 numbers. v30 status: MATCH 899, not checked against v30 278, NOT_IN_V30 149, OPEN 28. v28d status: MATCH 1149, v28f_change 116, OPEN 89.

## How to check one number

1. Find the row in the table below. 2. Open the file named in `where computed` (`file:function`). 3. Run `python paper.py build <unit>` (or open the unit notebook); the notebook prints the recomputed value. 4. `python tools/check_numbers.py --unit <unit> --manuscript-text visible_v30f.txt` compares printed and recomputed values and checks the v30 quote in its paragraph (the manuscript text is not part of this repository).
